- **Triage**: Capture rough ideas, bugs, and improvements as well-formed GitHub issues attached to a GitHub Project board, and transition existing items through their lifecycle. ([kin0992/dev-toolkit](https://github.com/kin0992/dev-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/project-manager/agents/triage.agent.md) · ★2)
- **triage**: Fast, cheap triage agent. Reads large inputs (scanner JSON, diffs, docs) and produces small structured summaries. Used as a pre-filter so expensive agents only run when needed and only see relevant data. Not invoked directly — called by pipeline commands. ([aitechnerd/ai-dev-team](https://github.com/aitechnerd/ai-dev-team/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage.md) · ★1)
- **triage**: Use proactively after an eval run completes with failures. Samples failing (and suspicious passing) trials from runs/&lt;ts&gt;/ and classifies each failure as agent-error, grader-error, or task-ambiguity using dual-trajectory evidence — the agent transcript read side by side with the grader's evidence and rationale. Excludes unverified trials (api_retry events, timeout exit 143) from classification and rates. Writes runs/&lt;ts&gt;/triage.md with per-failure classifications and a fix location (task, environment, or verifier) for each. Any grader-error finding requires an arm-evals re-run (suite version… ([Aznatkoiny/plugin-forge](https://github.com/Aznatkoiny/plugin-forge/blob/HEAD/agents/triage.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **triage**: Read-only second opinion on findings a reviewer already raised — an adversary's on a diff or a plan-adversary's on a brief — saying whether each holds, the disposition it recommends, and what a fix would touch. Dispatched by the conductor while disposing of a round. Never fixes, raises new findings or decides. ([dagarre00/claude-code-template](https://github.com/dagarre00/claude-code-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage.md) · ★1)
- **triage**: Proof Pursuit worker (blind, Phase 2A). The head dispatches it to rate the five branches ALGEBRAIC, TOPOLOGICAL, ANALYSIS, NUMBER-THEORY and DISCRETE for one cell (relevance, reason, entry point, risk) from the cell's structure and the obstacles so far, and to select the branches for Phase 2B. Never solves the cell and never sees proofs. Requires a prepared run/tasks/&lt;task-id&gt;/ folder with brief.md. ([defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam](https://github.com/defneirmakyurt/prooffinder_fam/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage.md) · ★1)

- **triage**: Triage phase of the SDD factory, launched by /sdd. Refines one issue before it enters the pipeline - completeness, correct Issue Type, duplicates, affected specs, path and size - and, above all, extracts every clarification the later phases would otherwise have to guess, while it is still cheap. Reads the repository, never modifies it; its only outputs are the marked triage comment and the issue's type. ([dneprokos/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/dneprokos/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/slack-bug-triage/agents/triage-bug-filer.md) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **triage-classifier**: Triage Classifier of the Software Factory. Use it first on every new request the Team Lead receives (except a plain status question) to classify the work - type, size, the lifecycle path, which conditional reviewers are needed, and whether it is clear enough to start - so small work exits early and the reviewer selection is deterministic. Read-only and fast; it never plans, designs or writes code. ([achisolomon/open-software-factory](https://github.com/achisolomon/open-software-factory/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/triage-classifier.md) · ★8)
- **triage-classifier**: Vendor reply parser + state-machine updater. Given pasted text from a platform (HackerOne / Bugcrowd / Intigriti / HITCON ZD / TWCERT / direct email), classify the triage outcome (N/A / Informative / Duplicate / Triaged / Resolved / Need-More-Info / Bounty-Awarded), extract bounty/CVSS/CVE if mentioned, propose Submission frontmatter patch, propose Kanban move, and surface KB-promotion candidates. Returns structured proposals — operator confirms before mutation. Use when user pastes a vendor reply or says "vendor replied", "triage result", "got N/A", "Accepted", "Duplicate", "bounty received"… ([guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework](https://github.com/guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/triage-classifier.md) · ★2)
- **triage-classifier**: Confirms or adjusts the computed review tier for a change using judgment about blast radius. Returns a structured review plan. Use as the first reasoning step in the code-review workflow. ([Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows](https://github.com/Tahmid12Khan/agentic-workflows/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-classifier.md))
- **triage-claude**: Assesses a CI failure diagnostic report and classifies the fix as easy or hard. Easy → outputs a targeted fix suggestion. Hard → signals that the investigate agent is required for root cause analysis. - **triage-contexte**: Dégrossissement de gros volumes. À utiliser dès qu'il faut consulter un fichier volumineux (logs, dump, JSON/CSV massif, build output) ou un dossier contenant beaucoup de fichiers. Lit, filtre, et ne renvoie que les extraits pertinents avec leurs chemins et numéros de ligne. - **triage-contexte**: Triage d'un gros fichier statique sur disque — log de plusieurs Mo, dump NDJSON/CSV/JSON massif, archive, sortie de build enregistrée. - **triage-labels**: The five-state vocabulary this repo uses for issue triage. Every issue carries exactly one state label. ([Integral-Productivity/software-architecture-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Integral-Productivity/software-architecture-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/triage-labels.md))

Triage adds exactly one issue-type label (`bug` or `enhancement`) and replaces `needs-triage` with exactly one readiness label when scope and verification are complete. - **triage-orchestrator**: Autonomous Jira issue triage - bulk categorization, label/component/priority suggestions, dedupe detection, linking issues to PRs and Confluence docs, batch transitions, and structured epic/sub-task creation. Triggers: 'triage incoming bugs', 'categorize unprocessed issues', 'find duplicates in this project', 'create epic with sub-tasks', 'batch-assign these issues'. ([Acendas/atlassian-suite](https://github.com/Acendas/atlassian-suite/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: Outer-loop triage orchestrator. Consolidates the discovery engine (discover-work.sh / loop-triage, T-017), the Operating-Loop telemetry roll-up (rollup-runs.sh, T-020), and cross-run failure clusters (cluster-failures.sh, T-044) into ONE de-duplicated, BUDGET-capped triage proposal file for a human to act on. Propose-only and manual-trigger — never edits the board. Use at the start of a cycle to decide what to pick up next. ([RipsawJP/shell-team](https://github.com/RipsawJP/shell-team/blob/HEAD/agents/triage-orchestrator.md))
- **triage-orchestrator**: 設定した条件に合う Coverity の警告を、複数のエージェントで並列に処置し、同じ原因の CID を統合して、すべての結果を別のエージェントに検証させる。`copilot --agent` で起動したメインの会話として使う。 ([xclang-brain/plugin-market-place](https://github.com/xclang-brain/plugin-market-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/coverity-triage-kit/com.github.copilot/agents/triage-orchestrator.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **triage-product**: Read-only triage perspective for incoming tasks. Challenges the user intent — who actually uses this, what problem it solves, what's in MVP, what edge cases break it, what success looks like. Invoked by /triage in parallel with triage-architect and triage-engineer. Returns a structured brief — never modifies files.
